# V2 Localized Copy Table (Draft)

## Purpose

Provide a single translation-ready source for user-facing strings in V2 screen specs.

## Locales

- Source locale: `en-US`
- Required locales: `pt-BR`, `es-ES`

## Key Format

- Pattern: `<screen>.<section>.<element>`
- Example: `auth.role.intro`

## Translation Rules

- Keep message meaning intact: professional connection is optional for student journey.
- Prefer plain language and action-first CTAs.
- Do not introduce domain jargon when a simple alternative exists.

## HouseGains v1 — In progress (copy proposal)

Picker and preview keys are present in all three HouseGains locale dictionaries. The remaining household, chore, XP, ranks, feed, and settings rows are proposed copy that still needs bundle wiring and screen-usage validation. This inventory is not release evidence.

## HouseGains v1 — Style picker pager keys

New keys for the horizontal style picker (task T2). They are added to all six bundles in `apps/mobile/localization/housegains/`, next to the other `housegains.appearance.*` keys. Values are exact. The existing `housegains.appearance.continue` and `select` keys are kept as-is in the table above.

| `housegains.appearance.next` | Picker | Next page button | Next style | Próximo estilo | Estilo siguiente | Style suivant | Nächster Stil | Stile successivo |  |

## HouseGains — Household admins, moderation and weekly standings keys

New keys for the moderation scope: household admins (creator grants or removes the admin role), member reports of completions, admin verdicts within the 72-hour window, and the Ranks "Last week" card from the latest closed week. They are added to all six bundles in `apps/mobile/localization/housegains/`. Source of truth: [gamification-moderation-contract.md](../../../../docs/implementation/gamification-moderation-contract.md) Parts A and B. Values are exact; named values in braces are interpolated.

| Key | Screen | Usage | en-US | pt-BR | es-ES | fr-FR | de-DE | it-IT | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `housegains.auth.sync.alreadyReported` | Sync error | reportCompletion duplicate | You already reported this chore. | Você já denunciou esta tarefa. | Ya reportaste esta tarea. | Tu as déjà signalé cette tâche. | Du hast diese Aufgabe bereits gemeldet. | Hai già segnalato questa attività. | |
| `housegains.auth.sync.alreadyReviewed` | Sync error | dispute already resolved | An admin already reviewed this chore. | Um admin já revisou esta tarefa. | Un admin ya revisó esta tarea. | Un admin a déjà examiné cette tâche. | Ein Admin hat diese Aufgabe bereits geprüft. | Un admin ha già esaminato questa attività. | |
| `housegains.auth.sync.reviewWindowClosed` | Sync error | 72-hour window closed | The 72-hour window to review this completion has closed. | O prazo de 72 horas para revisar esta conclusão já passou. | El plazo de 72 horas para revisar esta tarea ya pasó. | Le délai de 72 heures pour examiner cette tâche est dépassé. | Die 72-Stunden-Frist zum Prüfen dieser Aufgabe ist abgelaufen. | Le 72 ore per esaminare questa attività sono scadute. | |
| `housegains.auth.sync.conflictOfInterest` | Sync error | admin completed a target | You completed part of this chore, so another admin must review it. | Você concluiu parte desta tarefa, então outro admin deve revisá-la. | Completaste parte de esta tarea, así que otro admin debe revisarla. | Tu as terminé une partie de cette tâche, un autre admin doit donc l’examiner. | Du hast einen Teil dieser Aufgabe erledigt, daher muss ein anderer Admin sie prüfen. | Hai completato parte di questa attività, quindi un altro admin deve esaminarla. | Contract A2 conflict-of-interest rule. |
| `housegains.auth.sync.nothingToReport` | Sync error | no other-member completions | There is nothing completed by another member to report. | Não há conclusões de outras pessoas para denunciar. | No hay tareas de otras personas para reportar. | Il n’y a aucune tâche d’un autre membre à signaler. | Es gibt keine erledigten Aufgaben anderer Mitglieder zu melden. | Non ci sono attività completate da altri membri da segnalare. | |
| `housegains.screens.common.confirm` | Common | sheet confirm button | Confirm | Confirmar | Confirmar | Confirmer | Bestätigen | Conferma | |
| `housegains.screens.members.makeAdmin` | Settings people | grant admin role | Make admin | Tornar admin | Hacer admin | Nommer admin | Zum Admin machen | Rendi admin | Creator only (contract A1). |
| `housegains.screens.members.removeAdmin` | Settings people | remove admin role | Remove admin | Remover admin | Quitar admin | Retirer le rôle d’admin | Admin-Rolle entfernen | Rimuovi admin | Creator only (contract A1). |
| `housegains.screens.members.makeAdminTitle` | Settings people | confirm sheet title | Make {name} an admin? | Tornar {name} admin? | ¿Convertir a {name} en admin? | Nommer {name} admin ? | {name} zum Admin machen? | Rendere {name} admin? | Named values in braces are interpolated. |
| `housegains.screens.members.makeAdminBody` | Settings people | confirm sheet body | Admins review reported completions and can mark chores as not done. | Admins revisam conclusões denunciadas e podem marcar tarefas como não feitas. | Los admins revisan las tareas reportadas y pueden marcarlas como no hechas. | Les admins examinent les tâches signalées et peuvent les marquer comme non faites. | Admins prüfen gemeldete Aufgaben und können sie als nicht erledigt markieren. | Gli admin esaminano le attività segnalate e possono segnarle come non fatte. | |
| `housegains.screens.members.removeAdminTitle` | Settings people | confirm sheet title | Remove {name} as admin? | Remover {name} do cargo de admin? | ¿Quitar a {name} como admin? | Retirer le rôle d’admin à {name} ? | {name} als Admin entfernen? | Rimuovere {name} come admin? | Named values in braces are interpolated. |
| `housegains.screens.members.removeAdminBody` | Settings people | confirm sheet body | {name} keeps their points and history, but can no longer review reports. | {name} mantém os pontos e o histórico, mas não poderá mais revisar denúncias. | {name} conserva sus puntos y su historial, pero ya no podrá revisar reportes. | {name} garde ses points et son historique, mais ne peut plus examiner les signalements. | {name} behält Punkte und Verlauf, kann aber keine Meldungen mehr prüfen. | {name} mantiene i punti e lo storico, ma non potrà più esaminare le segnalazioni. | Named values in braces are interpolated. |
| `housegains.screens.settings.reports` | Settings | open reports entry | Completion reports | Conclusões denunciadas | Tareas reportadas | Tâches signalées | Gemeldete Aufgaben | Attività segnalate | Admins only, with a pending count badge. |

## HouseGains six-language source

The active HouseGains app supports Portuguese, Spanish, French, German and Italian, plus English. The complete generated [six-language copy table](../housegains-localized-copy.md) includes every string in all six locales; the older fitness-product rows above remain archived reference material.

## Notes

- All es-ES translations have been filled. Review with a native Spanish speaker before release.
- Keep this file aligned with screen specs and implementation keys.
- The HouseGains locale rows above match the shipped en-US, pt-BR, and es-ES dictionaries; source coverage is verified by `localization/housegains/catalog.test.ts`.
